\bm{n}\cdot I_{\delta}^{2}\nabla_{X}\Phi_{1}^{\mathrm{res,1}}=0&\mbox{on}\quad\Sigma_{1},\\
\bm{n}\cdot I_{\delta}^{2}\nabla_{X}\Phi_{2}^{\mathrm{res,1}}=\underline{h}_{2}r_{B}&\mbox{on}\quad\Sigma_{2},\\
\Phi_{\ell}^{\mathrm{res,1}}=0&\mbox{on}\quad\Gamma\qquad(\ell=1,2),\end{cases}

so that
(\Phi_{1}^{\mathrm{res,2}},\Phi_{2}^{\mathrm{res,2}})
satisfy

(8.4)

\begin{cases}\nabla_{X}\cdot I_{\delta}^{2}\nabla_{X}\Phi_{\ell}^{\mathrm{res,2}}=0&\mbox{in}\quad\Omega_{\ell}\qquad(\ell=1,2),\\
\bm{n}\cdot I_{\delta}^{2}\nabla_{X}\Phi_{\ell}^{\mathrm{res,2}}=0&\mbox{on}\quad\Sigma_{\ell}\qquad(\ell=1,2),\\
\bm{n}\cdot I_{\delta}^{2}\nabla_{X}\Phi_{2}^{\mathrm{res,2}}-\bm{n}\cdot I_{\delta}^{2}\nabla_{X}\Phi_{1}^{\mathrm{res,2}}=r_{S}&\mbox{on}\quad\Gamma,\\
\underline{\rho}_{2}\Phi_{2}^{\mathrm{res,2}}-\underline{\rho}_{1}\Phi_{1}^{\mathrm{res,2}}=0&\mbox{on}\quad\Gamma,\end{cases}

where we used the relations
\Lambda_{1}[\Phi_{1}^{\mathrm{res,2}}|_{z=\zeta}]=-\bm{n}\cdot I_{\delta}^{2}\nabla_{X}\Phi_{1}^{\mathrm{res,2}}

and
\Lambda_{2}[\Phi_{2}^{\mathrm{res,2}}|_{z=\zeta}]=\bm{n}\cdot I_{\delta}^{2}\nabla_{X}\Phi_{2}^{\mathrm{res,2}}
on
\Gamma
.
It is easy to see that

\displaystyle\|I_{\delta}\nabla_{X}\Phi_{1}^{\mathrm{res,1}}\|_{L^{2}(\Omega_{1})}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim(\underline{h}_{1}\delta)^{2}\|R_{1}\|_{L^{2}(\Omega_{1})}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\underline{h}_{1}(\underline{h}_{1}\delta)^{2}\|(r_{1,0},r_{1,1},\ldots,r_{1,2N+2})\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\underline{h}_{1}(\underline{h}_{1}\delta)^{2(4N+3)}\|\nabla\tilde{\bm{\phi}}_{1}\|_{H^{4N+3}}^{2}

and that

\displaystyle\|I_{\delta}\nabla_{X}\Phi_{2}^{\mathrm{res,1}}\|_{L^{2}(\Omega_{2})}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\underline{h}_{2}(\underline{h}_{2}\delta)^{2}(\underline{h}_{2}^{-1}\|R_{2}\|_{L^{2}(\Omega_{2})}^{2}+\|r_{B}\|_{L^{2}}^{2})

\displaystyle\lesssim\underline{h}_{2}(\underline{h}_{2}\delta)^{2}(\|(r_{2,0},r_{2,1},\ldots,r_{2,2N^{*}+2})\|_{L^{2}}+\|r_{B}\|_{L^{2}}^{2})

\displaystyle\lesssim\underline{h}_{2}(\underline{h}_{2}\delta)^{2(4N+3)}(\|\nabla\tilde{\bm{\phi}}_{2}\|_{H^{4N+3}}+\|\tilde{\bm{\phi}}_{2}^{\prime}\|_{H^{4N+3}}).

Therefore, by Lemma
5.1
together with (
2.15
) we have

\sum_{\ell=1,2}\underline{\rho}_{\ell}\|I_{\delta}\nabla_{X}\Phi_{\ell}^{\mathrm{res,1}}\|_{L^{2}(\Omega_{\ell})}^{2}\lesssim((\underline{h}_{1}\delta)^{4N+3}+(\underline{h}_{2}\delta)^{4N+3})^{2}\|\nabla\phi\|_{H^{4N+3}}^{2}.

On the other hand, it follows from Lemmas
8.1
,
4.5
,
7.2
,
and
5.1
that

\displaystyle\sum_{\ell=1,2}\underline{\rho}_{\ell}\|I_{\delta}\nabla_{X}\Phi_{\ell}^{\mathrm{res,2}}\|_{L^{2}(\Omega_{\ell})}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\min_{\ell=1,2}\frac{\underline{\rho}_{\ell}}{\underline{h}_{\ell}}\|((-\Delta)^{-\frac{1}{2}}+\underline{h}_{\ell}\delta)r_{S}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{\ell=1,2}\frac{\underline{\rho}_{\ell}}{\underline{h}_{\ell}}\|((-\Delta)^{-\frac{1}{2}}+\underline{h}_{\ell}\delta)(\underline{h}_{\ell}\Lambda_{\ell}^{(2N+2)}-\Lambda_{\ell})[\tilde{\Phi}_{\ell}^{\mathrm{app}}|_{z=\zeta}]\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{\ell=1,2}\underline{\rho}_{\ell}\underline{h}_{\ell}(\underline{h}_{\ell}\delta)^{2(4N+2)}\|\nabla(\tilde{\Phi}_{\ell}^{\mathrm{app}}|_{z=\zeta})\|_{H^{4N+3}}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{\ell=1,2}\underline{\rho}_{\ell}\underline{h}_{\ell}(\underline{h}_{\ell}\delta)^{2(4N+2)}(\|\nabla\tilde{\bm{\phi}}_{\ell}\|_{H^{4N+3}}^{2}+\|\tilde{\bm{\phi}}_{\ell}^{\prime}\|_{H^{4N+3}}^{2})

\displaystyle\lesssim((\underline{h}_{1}\delta)^{4N+2}+(\underline{h}_{2}\delta)^{4N+2})^{2}\|\nabla\phi\|_{H^{4N+3}}^{2}.

Summarizing the above estimates, we obtain

|I_{1}|\lesssim((\underline{h}_{1}\delta)^{4N+2}+(\underline{h}_{2}\delta)^{4N+2})\|\nabla\phi\|_{H^{4N+3}}\|\nabla\phi\|_{L^{2}}
.

We proceed to evaluate
I_{2}
, which can be written as

\displaystyle I_{2}

\displaystyle=\sum_{\ell=1,2}\underline{\rho}_{\ell}\underline{h}_{\ell}(L_{\ell}^{(2N+2)}\tilde{\bm{\phi}}_{\ell},\tilde{\bm{\phi}}_{\ell})_{L^{2}}-\sum_{\ell=1,2}\underline{\rho}_{\ell}\underline{h}_{\ell}(L_{\ell}^{(N)}\bm{\phi}_{\ell},\bm{\phi}_{\ell})_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle=:I_{2,1}+I_{2,2}.

In view of (
3.8
), we see that

\displaystyle I_{2,1}

\displaystyle=\underline{\rho}_{1}\underline{h}_{1}(\mathcal{L}_{1,0}^{(2N+2)}\tilde{\bm{\phi}}_{1},\bm{l}_{1}^{(2N+2)}\cdot\tilde{\bm{\phi}}_{1})_{L^{2}}+\underline{\rho}_{2}\underline{h}_{2}(\mathcal{L}_{2,0}^{(2N+2)}\tilde{\bm{\phi}}_{2},\bm{l}_{2}^{(2N+2)}\cdot\tilde{\bm{\phi}}_{2})_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle=(\underline{h}_{2}\mathcal{L}_{2,0}^{(2N+2)}\tilde{\bm{\phi}}_{2},\underline{\rho}_{2}\bm{l}_{2}^{(2N+2)}\cdot\tilde{\bm{\phi}}_{2}-\underline{\rho}_{1}\bm{l}_{1}^{(2N+2)}\cdot\tilde{\bm{\phi}}_{1})_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle=(\underline{h}_{2}\mathcal{L}_{2,0}^{(2N+2)}\tilde{\bm{\phi}}_{2},\phi)_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle=(\underline{h}_{2}\mathcal{L}_{2,0}^{(2N+2)}\tilde{\bm{\phi}}_{2},\underline{\rho}_{2}\bm{l}_{2}^{(N)}\cdot\bm{\phi}_{2}-\underline{\rho}_{1}\bm{l}_{1}^{(N)}\cdot\bm{\phi}_{1})_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle=\underline{\rho}_{1}\underline{h}_{1}(\mathcal{L}_{1,0}^{(2N+2)}\tilde{\bm{\phi}}_{1},\bm{l}_{1}^{(N)}\cdot\bm{\phi}_{1})_{L^{2}}+\underline{\rho}_{2}\underline{h}_{2}(\mathcal{L}_{2,0}^{(2N+2)}\tilde{\bm{\phi}}_{2},\bm{l}_{2}^{(N)}\cdot\bm{\phi}_{2})_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle=\underline{\rho}_{1}\underline{h}_{1}\sum_{i=0}^{N}\sum_{j=0}^{2N+2}(L_{1,ij}\tilde{\phi}_{1,j},\phi_{1,i})_{L^{2}}+\underline{\rho}_{2}\underline{h}_{2}\sum_{i=0}^{N^{*}}\sum_{j=0}^{2N^{*}+2}(L_{2,ij}\tilde{\phi}_{2,j},\phi_{2,i})_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle=\underline{\rho}_{1}\underline{h}_{1}\sum_{i=0}^{N}\sum_{j=0}^{2N+2}(L_{1,ji}\phi_{1,i},\tilde{\phi}_{1,j})_{L^{2}}+\underline{\rho}_{2}\underline{h}_{2}\sum_{i=0}^{N^{*}}\sum_{j=0}^{2N^{*}+2}(L_{2,ji}\phi_{2,i},\tilde{\phi}_{2,j})_{L^{2}},

where we used
L_{\ell,ij}^{*}=L_{\ell,ji}
.
Similarly, we see also that

\displaystyle I_{2,2}

\displaystyle=\underline{\rho}_{1}\underline{h}_{1}(\mathcal{L}_{1,0}^{(N)}\bm{\phi}_{1},\bm{l}_{1}^{(N)}\cdot\bm{\phi}_{1})_{L^{2}}+\underline{\rho}_{2}\underline{h}_{2}(\mathcal{L}_{2,0}^{(N)}\bm{\phi}_{2},\bm{l}_{2}^{(N)}\cdot\bm{\phi}_{2})_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle=(\underline{h}_{2}\mathcal{L}_{2,0}^{(N)}\bm{\phi}_{2},\underline{\rho}_{2}\bm{l}_{2}^{(N)}\cdot\bm{\phi}_{2}-\underline{\rho}_{1}\bm{l}_{1}^{(N)}\cdot\bm{\phi}_{1})_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle=(\underline{h}_{2}\mathcal{L}_{2,0}^{(N)}\bm{\phi}_{2},\phi)_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle=(\underline{h}_{2}\mathcal{L}_{2,0}^{(N)}\bm{\phi}_{2},\underline{\rho}_{2}\bm{l}_{2}^{(2N+2)}\cdot\tilde{\bm{\phi}}_{2}-\underline{\rho}_{1}\bm{l}_{1}^{(2N+2)}\cdot\tilde{\bm{\phi}}_{1})_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle=\underline{\rho}_{1}\underline{h}_{1}(\mathcal{L}_{1,0}^{(N)}\bm{\phi}_{1},\bm{l}_{1}^{(2N+2)}\cdot\tilde{\bm{\phi}}_{1})_{L^{2}}+\underline{\rho}_{2}\underline{h}_{2}(\mathcal{L}_{2,0}^{(N)}\bm{\phi}_{2},\bm{l}_{2}^{(2N+2)}\cdot\tilde{\bm{\phi}}_{2})_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle=\underline{\rho}_{1}\underline{h}_{1}\sum_{j=0}^{2N+2}(H_{1}^{2j}\mathcal{L}_{1,0}^{(N)}\bm{\phi}_{1},\tilde{\phi}_{1,j})_{L^{2}}+\underline{\rho}_{2}\underline{h}_{2}\sum_{j=0}^{2N^{*}+2}(H_{2}^{p_{j}}\mathcal{L}_{2,0}^{(N)}\bm{\phi}_{2},\tilde{\phi}_{2,j})_{L^{2}}.

Here, it follows from (
3.8
) that
H_{1}^{2j}\mathcal{L}_{1,0}^{(N)}\bm{\phi}_{1}=\sum_{i=0}^{N}L_{1,ji}\phi_{1,i}

and
H_{2}^{p_{j}}\mathcal{L}_{2,0}^{(N)}\bm{\phi}_{1}=\sum_{i=0}^{N^{*}}L_{2,ji}\phi_{2,i}
hold only for
j=0,1,\ldots,N
and for

j=0,1,\ldots,N^{*}
, respectively.
Therefore, we have

\displaystyle I_{2,2}

\displaystyle=\underline{\rho}_{1}\underline{h}_{1}\sum_{i=0}^{N}\sum_{j=0}^{N}(L_{1,ji}\phi_{1,i},\tilde{\phi}_{1,j})_{L^{2}}+\underline{\rho}_{2}\underline{h}_{2}\sum_{i=0}^{N^{*}}\sum_{j=0}^{N^{*}}(L_{2,ji}\phi_{2,i},\tilde{\phi}_{2,j})_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\quad\;+\underline{\rho}_{1}\underline{h}_{1}\sum_{i=0}^{N}\sum_{j=N+1}^{2N+2}(H_{1}^{2j}L_{1,0i}\phi_{1,i},\tilde{\phi}_{1,j})_{L^{2}}+\underline{\rho}_{2}\underline{h}_{2}\sum_{i=0}^{N^{*}}\sum_{j=N^{*}+1}^{2N^{*}+2}(H_{2}^{p_{j}}L_{2,0i}\phi_{2,i},\tilde{\phi}_{2,j})_{L^{2}},

so that

\displaystyle I_{2}

\displaystyle=\underline{\rho}_{1}\underline{h}_{1}\sum_{i=0}^{N}\sum_{j=N+1}^{2N+2}((L_{1,ji}-H_{1}^{2j}L_{1,0i})\phi_{1,i},\tilde{\phi}_{1,j})_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\quad\;+\underline{\rho}_{2}\underline{h}_{2}\sum_{i=0}^{N^{*}}\sum_{j=N^{*}+1}^{2N^{*}+2}((L_{2,ji}-H_{2}^{p_{j}}L_{2,0i})\phi_{2,i},\tilde{\phi}_{2,j})_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle=\underline{\rho}_{1}\underline{h}_{1}\sum_{i=0}^{N}\sum_{j=N+1}^{2N+2}((L_{1,ji}-H_{1}^{2j}L_{1,0i})(\phi_{1,i}-\tilde{\phi}_{1,i}),\tilde{\phi}_{1,j})_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\quad\;+\underline{\rho}_{2}\underline{h}_{2}\sum_{i=0}^{N^{*}}\sum_{j=N^{*}+1}^{2N^{*}+2}((L_{2,ji}-H_{2}^{p_{j}}L_{2,0i})(\phi_{2,i}-\tilde{\phi}_{2,i}),\tilde{\phi}_{2,j})_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\quad\;-\underline{\rho}_{1}\underline{h}_{1}\sum_{i=N+1}^{2N+2}\sum_{j=N+1}^{2N+2}((L_{1,ji}-H_{1}^{2j}L_{1,0i})\tilde{\phi}_{1,i},\tilde{\phi}_{1,j})_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\quad\;-\underline{\rho}_{2}\underline{h}_{2}\sum_{i=N^{*}+1}^{2N^{*}+2}\sum_{j=N^{*}+1}^{2N^{*}+2}((L_{2,ji}-H_{2}^{p_{j}}L_{2,0i})\tilde{\phi}_{2,i},\tilde{\phi}_{2,j})_{L^{2}}.

Hence, denoting by
\bm{\varphi}_{1}=(\varphi_{1,0},\varphi_{1,1},\ldots,\varphi_{1,N})^{\mathrm{T}}
and

\bm{\varphi}_{2}=(\varphi_{2,0},\varphi_{2,1},\ldots,\varphi_{2,N^{*}})^{\mathrm{T}}
with

\varphi_{\ell,i}=\phi_{\ell,i}-\tilde{\phi}_{\ell,i}
we obtain

\displaystyle|I_{2}|

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{\ell=1,2}\underline{\rho}_{\ell}\underline{h}_{\ell}(\|\nabla\bm{\varphi}_{\ell}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+(\underline{h}_{\ell}\delta)^{-2}\|\bm{\varphi}_{\ell}^{\prime}\|_{L^{2}}^{2})

\displaystyle\quad\;+\underline{\rho}_{1}\underline{h}_{1}\|(\tilde{\phi}_{1,N+1},\tilde{\phi}_{1,N+2},\ldots,\tilde{\phi}_{1,2N+2})\|_{H^{1}}^{2}

\displaystyle\quad\;+\underline{\rho}_{2}\underline{h}_{2}\|(\tilde{\phi}_{2,N^{*}+1},\tilde{\phi}_{2,N^{*}+2},\ldots,\tilde{\phi}_{2,2N^{*}+2})\|_{H^{1}}^{2}

\displaystyle\quad\;+\underline{\rho}_{1}\underline{h}_{1}(\underline{h}_{1}\delta)^{-2}\|(\tilde{\phi}_{1,N+1},\tilde{\phi}_{1,N+2},\ldots,\tilde{\phi}_{1,2N+2})\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle\quad\;+\underline{\rho}_{2}\underline{h}_{2}(\underline{h}_{2}\delta)^{-2}\|(\tilde{\phi}_{2,N^{*}+1},\tilde{\phi}_{2,N^{*}+2},\ldots,\tilde{\phi}_{2,2N^{*}+2})\|_{L^{2}}^{2}.

Here, we note that
(\bm{\varphi}_{1},\bm{\varphi}_{2})
satisfy

\begin{cases}\mathcal{L}_{1,i}^{(N)}\bm{\varphi}_{1}=r_{1,i}\quad\mbox{for}\quad i=0,1,\ldots,N,\\
\mathcal{L}_{2,i}^{(N)}\bm{\varphi}_{2}=r_{2,i}\quad\mbox{for}\quad i=0,1,\ldots,N^{*},\\
\underline{h}_{1}\mathcal{L}_{1,0}^{(N)}\bm{\varphi}_{1}+\underline{h}_{2}\mathcal{L}_{2,0}^{(N)}\bm{\varphi}_{2}=\nabla\cdot(\underline{h}_{1}\bm{r}_{3,1}+\underline{h}_{2}\bm{r}_{3,2}),\\
\underline{\rho}_{2}\bm{l}_{2}^{(N)}\cdot\bm{\varphi}_{2}-\underline{\rho}_{1}\bm{l}_{1}^{(N)}\cdot\bm{\varphi}_{1}=\underline{\rho}_{1}r_{4,1}+\underline{\rho}_{2}r_{4,2},\end{cases}

where

\begin{cases}r_{1,i}=-\sum_{j=N+1}^{2N+2}(L_{1,ij}-H^{2i}L_{1,0j})\tilde{\phi}_{1,j}\quad\mbox{for}\quad i=0,1,\ldots,N,\\
r_{2,i}=-\sum_{j=N^{*}+1}^{2N^{*}+2}(L_{2,ij}-H^{p_{i}}L_{2,0j})\tilde{\phi}_{2,j}\quad\mbox{for}\quad i=0,1,\ldots,N^{*},\\
\nabla\cdot\bm{r}_{3,1}=\sum_{j=N+1}^{2N+2}L_{1,0j}\tilde{\phi}_{1,j},\quad\nabla\cdot\bm{r}_{3,2}=\sum_{j=N^{*}+1}^{2N^{*}+2}L_{2,0j}\tilde{\phi}_{2,j},\\
r_{4,1}=\sum_{j=N+1}^{2N+2}H_{1}^{2j}\tilde{\phi}_{1,j},\quad r_{4,2}=-\sum_{j=N^{*}+1}^{2N^{*}+2}H_{2}^{p_{j}}\tilde{\phi}_{2,j}.\end{cases}

We put
\bm{r}_{1}^{\prime}=(0,r_{1,1},\ldots,r_{1,N})^{\mathrm{T}}
and
\bm{r}_{2}^{\prime}=(0,r_{2,1},\ldots,r_{2,N})^{\mathrm{T}}
.
Then, with a suitable decomposition

\bm{r}_{\ell}=\bm{r}_{\ell}^{\mathrm{high}}+(\underline{h}_{\ell}\delta)^{-2}\bm{r}_{\ell}^{\mathrm{low}}
for
\ell=1,2
,
and using the linearity of (
5.1
), we see by Lemma
5.1
that

\displaystyle\sum_{\ell=1,2}\underline{\rho}_{\ell}\underline{h}_{\ell}(\|\nabla\bm{\varphi}_{\ell}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+(\underline{h}_{\ell}\delta)^{-2}\|\bm{\varphi}_{\ell}^{\prime}\|_{L^{2}}^{2})

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{\ell=1,2}\underline{\rho}_{\ell}\underline{h}_{\ell}(\|\bm{r}_{\ell}^{\mathrm{high}}\|_{H^{-1}}^{2}+(\underline{h}_{\ell}\delta)^{-2}\|\bm{r}_{\ell}^{\mathrm{low}}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|\bm{r}_{3,\ell}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|r_{4,\ell}\|_{H^{1}}^{2})

\displaystyle\lesssim\underline{\rho}_{1}\underline{h}_{1}\|(\tilde{\phi}_{1,N+1},\tilde{\phi}_{1,N+2},\ldots,\tilde{\phi}_{1,2N+2})\|_{H^{1}}^{2}

\displaystyle\quad\;+\underline{\rho}_{2}\underline{h}_{2}\|(\tilde{\phi}_{2,N^{*}+1},\tilde{\phi}_{2,N^{*}+2},\ldots,\tilde{\phi}_{2,2N^{*}+2})\|_{H^{1}}^{2}

\displaystyle\quad\;+\underline{\rho}_{1}\underline{h}_{1}(\underline{h}_{1}\delta)^{-2}\|(\tilde{\phi}_{1,N+1},\tilde{\phi}_{1,N+2},\ldots,\tilde{\phi}_{1,2N+2})\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle\quad\;+\underline{\rho}_{2}\underline{h}_{2}(\underline{h}_{2}\delta)^{-2}\|(\tilde{\phi}_{2,N^{*}+1},\tilde{\phi}_{2,N^{*}+2},\ldots,\tilde{\phi}_{2,2N^{*}+2})\|_{L^{2}}^{2}.

Moreover, it follows from
[
20
, Lemmas 5.2 and 5.4]
that

\displaystyle\|(\tilde{\phi}_{1,N+1},\tilde{\phi}_{1,N+2},\ldots,\tilde{\phi}_{1,2N+2})\|_{H^{k}}\lesssim(\underline{h}_{1}\delta)^{2N+2-k}\|\nabla\tilde{\bm{\phi}}_{1}\|_{H^{2N+1}}

\displaystyle\|(\tilde{\phi}_{2,N^{*}+1},\tilde{\phi}_{2,N^{*}+2},\ldots,\tilde{\phi}_{2,2N^{*}+2})\|_{H^{k}}\lesssim(\underline{h}_{2}\delta)^{2N+2-k}(\|\nabla\tilde{\bm{\phi}}_{2}\|_{H^{2N+1}}+\|\tilde{\bm{\phi}}_{2}^{\prime}\|_{H^{2N+1}}),

for
k=0,2
, and hence also for
k=1
by interpolation, so that

\displaystyle|I_{2}|

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{\ell=1,2}\underline{\rho}_{\ell}\underline{h}_{\ell}(\underline{h}_{\ell}\delta)^{4N+2}(\|\nabla\tilde{\bm{\phi}}_{\ell}\|_{H^{2N+1}}^{2}+\|\tilde{\bm{\phi}}_{\ell}^{\prime}\|_{H^{2N+1}}^{2})

\displaystyle\lesssim((\underline{h}_{1}\delta)^{4N+2}+(\underline{h}_{2}\delta)^{4N+2})\|\nabla\phi\|_{H^{2N+1}}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim((\underline{h}_{1}\delta)^{4N+2}+(\underline{h}_{2}\delta)^{4N+2})\|\nabla\phi\|_{H^{4N+2}}\|\nabla\phi\|_{L^{2}},

where we used Lemma
5.1
with (
2.15
), and interpolation.
This completes the proof of Theorem
3.10
.
